robust furniture that meets the
real-world need for humanizing
challenging environments.
Norix is not just a corporation, we’re a family. We care
about the people we serve, our community and our team.
We create contemporary designs that humanize our
industries without sacrificing robust structural integrity.
Our products are built with the wellbeing of the people
who use the furniture in mind, as well as the people
around them.
